We further demonstrate a mitigation strategy based on one-shot instruction reshaping using a Chain-of-Thought reasoning model, indicating that even subtle changes in wording and structure of system instructions can significantly reduce attack success rate without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [113] [VibeGuard: A Security Gate Framework for AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01052)
*Ying Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VibeGuard：针对AI辅助编程（vibe coding）引入的新型安全漏洞的预发布安全门控工具，在实验中实现了100%召回率和89.47%精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编程（vibe coding）的快速普及带来了新型安全漏洞，如Claude Code CLI泄露512,000行专有TypeScript代码的事件所示。现有静态分析和秘密扫描工具无法覆盖这些新型漏洞，存在检测盲区。

Method: 开发了VibeGuard预发布安全门控工具，专门针对五个检测盲区：工件卫生、打包配置漂移、源码映射暴露、硬编码密钥和供应链风险。在八个合成项目（七个有漏洞，一个干净对照）上进行了受控实验。

Result: VibeGuard实现了100%召回率、89.47%精度（F1=94.44%），在三个策略级别上对所有八个项目做出了正确的通过/失败门控决策。

Conclusion: VibeGuard填补了AI辅助编程引入的新型漏洞与现有安全工具检测能力之间的差距，为依赖AI代码生成的团队提供了深度防御工作流程的参考。

Abstract: "Vibe coding," in which developers delegate code generation to AI assistants and accept the output with little manual review, has gained rapid adoption in production settings. On March 31, 2026, Anthropic's Claude Code CLI shipped a 59.8 MB source map file in its npm package, exposing roughly 512,000 lines of proprietary TypeScript. The tool had itself been largely vibe-coded, and the leak traced to a misconfigured packaging rule rather than a logic bug. Existing static-analysis and secret-scanning tools did not cover this failure mode, pointing to a gap between the vulnerabilities AI tends to introduce and the vulnerabilities current tooling is built to find. We present VibeGuard, a pre-publish security gate that targets five such blind spots: artifact hygiene, packaging-configuration drift, source-map exposure, hardcoded secrets, and supply-chain risk. In controlled experiments on eight synthetic projects (seven vulnerable, one clean control), VibeGuard achieved 100% recall, 89.47% precision (F1 = 94.44%), and correct pass/fail gate decisions on all eight projects across three policy levels. We discuss how these results inform a defense-in-depth workflow for teams that rely on AI code generation.

</details>


### [114] [Obfuscating Code Vulnerabilities against Static Analysis in JavaScript Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01131)
*Francesco Pagano,Lorenzo Pisu,Leonardo Regano,Davide Maiorca,Alessio Merlo,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文实证评估了JavaScript代码混淆对SAST工具检测漏洞能力的影响，发现即使单一混淆技术也能显著降低漏洞检测率，而组合混淆技术几乎能完全规避检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混淆在保护知识产权的同时，也为攻击者提供了隐藏恶意代码的手段。在软件供应链中，单个受污染的包可能影响数千个应用，因此需要评估CI/CD管道依赖的SAST工具在面对混淆代码时的鲁棒性。

Method: 定义了现实的供应链威胁模型，引入漏洞检测损失(VDL)指标，进行两阶段研究：首先分析16个OWASP目录中的Node.js Web应用，然后扩展到260个GitHub上的实际项目。应用8种语义保持的混淆技术及其组合，评估Njsscan和Bearer两个代表性SAST工具。

Result: 单一混淆技术通常能抑制大多数基线发现（包括高危问题），而组合混淆技术能实现近乎完全的规避，VDL经常接近100%。结果表明当前JavaScript SAST对常见混淆技术基本没有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 当前JavaScript SAST工具在面对混淆代码时存在根本性缺陷，"干净"的扫描报告可能只提供虚假的安全感。论文讨论了实用的缓解指南和面向混淆感知分析的方向。

Abstract: Code obfuscation is widely adopted in modern software development to protect intellectual property and hinder reverse engineering, but it also provides attackers with a powerful means to conceal malicious logic inside otherwise legitimate JavaScript code. In a software supply chain where a single compromised package can affect thousands of applications, this raises a critical question: how robust are the Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools that CI/CD pipelines rely on as automated security gatekeepers? This paper answers that question by empirically quantifying the impact of JavaScript obfuscation on state-of-practice SAST. We define a realistic supply-chain threat model in which an adversary injects vulnerable code and iteratively obfuscates it until the pipeline reports a clean scan. To measure the resulting degradation, we introduce the Vulnerability Detection Loss (VDL) metric and conduct a two-phase study. First, we analyze 16 vulnerable-by-design Node.js web applications from the OWASP directory; second, we extend the analysis to 260 in-the-wild JavaScript/Node.js projects from GitHub. Across both datasets, we apply eight semantics-preserving obfuscation techniques and their combinations and evaluate two representative SAST tools, Njsscan and Bearer. Even a single obfuscation technique typically suppresses most baseline findings, including high-severity issues, while stacking techniques yield near-total evasion, with VDL often approaching 100%. Our results show that current JavaScript SAST is fundamentally not robust against commonplace obfuscations and that "clean" reports on obfuscated code may offer only a false sense of security. Finally, we discuss practical mitigation guidelines and directions for obfuscation-aware analysis.

</details>


### [115] [AgentWatcher: A Rule-based Prompt Injection Monitor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01194)
*Yanting Wang,Wei Zou,Runpeng Geng,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentWatcher：一种可扩展、可解释的提示注入检测方法，通过归因分析和规则推理解决长上下文检测效果下降和决策不透明的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入检测方法存在两个主要局限：1）随着上下文长度增加，检测效果显著下降；2）缺乏明确定义提示注入的规则，导致检测决策不透明、难以解释。这些局限使得LLM及其应用（如智能体）在面对提示注入攻击时非常脆弱。

Method: AgentWatcher采用双管齐下的方法：1）通过归因分析将LLM输出归因到少量因果影响大的上下文片段，使检测聚焦于相对较短的文本，从而可扩展到长上下文；2）定义一组明确规则来界定什么是/不是提示注入，并使用监控LLM基于归因文本进行规则推理，使检测决策更可解释。

Result: 在工具使用智能体基准测试和长上下文理解数据集上的综合评估表明，AgentWatcher能有效检测提示注入，并在无攻击时保持实用性。该方法解决了现有方法的局限性，提供了可扩展且可解释的检测方案。

Conclusion: AgentWatcher通过归因分析和基于规则的推理，解决了现有提示注入检测方法在长上下文下的可扩展性和决策透明度问题，为LLM及其应用提供了更可靠的提示注入防御机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and their applications, such as agents, are highly vulnerable to prompt injection attacks. State-of-the-art prompt injection detection methods have the following limitations: (1) their effectiveness degrades significantly as context length increases, and (2) they lack explicit rules that define what constitutes prompt injection, causing detection decisions to be implicit, opaque, and difficult to reason about. In this work, we propose AgentWatcher to address the above two limitations. To address the first limitation, AgentWatcher attributes the LLM's output (e.g., the action of an agent) to a small set of causally influential context segments. By focusing detection on a relatively short text, AgentWatcher can be scalable to long contexts. To address the second limitation, we define a set of rules specifying what does and does not constitute a prompt injection, and use a monitor LLM to reason over these rules based on the attributed text, making the detection decisions more explainable. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation on tool-use agent benchmarks and long-context understanding datasets. The experimental results demonstrate that AgentWatcher can effectively detect prompt injection and maintain utility without attacks. The code is available at https://github.com/wang-yanting/AgentWatcher.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Forecast collapse of transformer-based models under squared loss in financial time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00064)
*Pierre Andreoletti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究分析了在平方损失下对弱条件结构时间序列进行轨迹预测时，高表达能力模型的表现。研究发现，在条件期望退化的机制下，增加模型表达能力不会改善预测精度，反而会引入围绕最优预测器的虚假轨迹波动，导致预测方差增加而偏差不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解释Transformer等表达能力强的模型在金融时间序列预测中表现不佳的现象。当时间序列具有弱条件结构且条件期望退化时，理论上最优预测器是平凡的（如价格预测为平坦线，收益率预测为零），但高表达能力模型会引入不必要的波动。

Method: 方法基于平方损失风险最小化的经典特征分析，研究条件期望退化的机制。通过理论分析表明，在这种机制下，增加模型表达能力会重用噪声并产生虚假轨迹波动。同时使用高频欧元/美元汇率数据进行数值实验，分析轨迹级预测误差的分布。

Result: 理论分析表明，在条件期望退化机制下，增加模型表达能力会增加预测方差而不减少偏差。数值实验显示，Transformer模型在大多数预测窗口上的误差大于简单的线性基准模型，这与理论识别的方差驱动机制一致。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论：在弱条件结构时间序列的轨迹预测中，当条件期望退化时，高表达能力模型（如Transformer）不会改善预测精度，反而会因引入虚假波动而增加预测方差，导致性能下降。这解释了Transformer在金融时间序列预测中表现不佳的现象。

Abstract: We study trajectory forecasting under squared loss for time series with weak conditional structure, using highly expressive prediction models. Building on the classical characterization of squared-loss risk minimization, we emphasize regimes in which the conditional expectation of future trajectories is effectively degenerate, leading to trivial Bayes-optimal predictors (flat for prices and zero for returns in standard financial settings).  In this regime, increased model expressivity does not improve predictive accuracy but instead introduces spurious trajectory fluctuations around the optimal predictor. These fluctuations arise from the reuse of noise and result in increased prediction variance without any reduction in bias.  This provides a process-level explanation for the degradation of Transformerbased forecasts on financial time series.  We complement these theoretical results with numerical experiments on high-frequency EUR/USD exchange rate data, analyzing the distribution of trajectory-level forecasting errors. The results show that Transformer-based models yield larger errors than a simple linear benchmark on a large majority of forecasting windows, consistent with the variance-driven mechanism identified by the theory.

</details>


### [117] [Isomorphic Functionalities between Ant Colony and Ensemble Learning: Part II-On the Strength of Weak Learnability and the Boosting Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00038)
*Ernest Fokoué,Gregory Babbitt,Yuval Levental*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文建立了蚁群决策与AdaBoost算法之间的数学同构，证明自适应权重调整是生物与计算系统中偏差减少的共同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在第一部分证明了蚁群决策与随机森林在方差减少方面的同构性后，本文旨在研究偏差减少的互补机制，探索蚁群自适应招募与Boosting算法之间的数学对应关系。

Method: 建立了AdaBoost自适应重加权与蚁群信息素介导招募之间的形式化映射，证明了弱可学习性基本定理在群体决策中的对应关系，并通过综合模拟验证理论。

Result: 证明了蚁群自适应招募与Boosting算法在偏差减少方面具有相同的数学结构，Boosting的边界理论对应群体决策的稳定性，蚁群通过自适应招募可获得与Boosting算法相同的偏差减少效益。

Conclusion: 完成了集成智能的统一理论，揭示了方差减少（第一部分）和偏差减少（第二部分）都是生物与计算系统中集体智能相同底层数学原理的表现形式。

Abstract: In Part I of this series, we established a rigorous mathematical isomorphism between ant colony decision-making and random forest learning, demonstrating that variance reduction through decorrelation is a universal principle shared by biological and computational ensembles. Here we turn to the complementary mechanism: bias reduction through adaptive weighting. Just as boosting algorithms sequentially focus on difficult instances, ant colonies dynamically amplify successful foraging paths through pheromone-mediated recruitment. We prove that these processes are mathematically isomorphic, establishing that the fundamental theorem of weak learnability has a direct analog in colony decision-making. We develop a formal mapping between AdaBoost's adaptive reweighting and ant recruitment dynamics, show that the margin theory of boosting corresponds to the stability of quorum decisions, and demonstrate through comprehensive simulation that ant colonies implementing adaptive recruitment achieve the same bias-reduction benefits as boosting algorithms. This completes a unified theory of ensemble intelligence, revealing that both variance reduction (Part I) and bias reduction (Part II) are manifestations of the same underlying mathematical principles governing collective intelligence in biological and computational systems.

</details>


### [118] [Scaled Gradient Descent for Ill-Conditioned Low-Rank Matrix Recovery with Optimal Sampling Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00060)
*Zhenxuan Li,Meng Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过精细分析证明，ScaledGD方法在低秩矩阵恢复问题中同时实现了最优样本复杂度O((n₁+n₂)r)和改进的迭代复杂度O(log(1/ε))，突破了传统方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩矩阵恢复问题中，传统梯度下降方法存在两个关键限制：次优的样本复杂度O((n₁+n₂)r²)和依赖于条件数的迭代复杂度O(κlog(1/ε))，导致在病态矩阵上收敛缓慢。虽然ScaledGD改进了迭代复杂度，但样本复杂度仍为次优；而标准GD在PSD设置下通过虚拟序列技术达到了最优样本复杂度，但迭代复杂度较差。需要一种方法同时实现最优样本复杂度和快速收敛。

Method: 采用更精细的分析方法，证明ScaledGD（缩放梯度下降）在一般低秩矩阵恢复问题中同时实现最优样本复杂度和改进的迭代复杂度。该方法超越了PSD设置的限制，适用于更广泛的低秩矩阵恢复问题。

Result: 理论分析表明ScaledGD实现了最优样本复杂度O((n₁+n₂)r)和快速迭代复杂度O(log(1/ε))，数值实验进一步验证了该方法在病态矩阵上的加速收敛性能，同时保持了最优采样复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过精细分析，ScaledGD成功克服了传统方法的局限性，在低秩矩阵恢复问题中同时实现了最优样本复杂度和快速收敛，为病态矩阵的高效恢复提供了理论保证和实用方法。

Abstract: The low-rank matrix recovery problem seeks to reconstruct an unknown $n_1 \times n_2$ rank-$r$ matrix from $m$ linear measurements, where $m\ll n_1n_2$. This problem has been extensively studied over the past few decades, leading to a variety of algorithms with solid theoretical guarantees. Among these, gradient descent based non-convex methods have become particularly popular due to their computational efficiency. However, these methods typically suffer from two key limitations: a sub-optimal sample complexity of $O((n_1 + n_2)r^2)$ and an iteration complexity of $O(κ\log(1/ε))$ to achieve $ε$-accuracy, resulting in slow convergence when the target matrix is ill-conditioned. Here, $κ$ denotes the condition number of the unknown matrix. Recent studies show that a preconditioned variant of GD, known as scaled gradient descent (ScaledGD), can significantly reduce the iteration complexity to $O(\log(1/ε))$. Nonetheless, its sample complexity remains sub-optimal at $O((n_1 + n_2)r^2)$. In contrast, a delicate virtual sequence technique demonstrates that the standard GD in the positive semidefinite (PSD) setting achieves the optimal sample complexity $O((n_1 + n_2)r)$, but converges more slowly with an iteration complexity $O(κ^2 \log(1/ε))$. In this paper, through a more refined analysis, we show that ScaledGD achieves both the optimal sample complexity $O((n_1 + n_2)r)$ and the improved iteration complexity $O(\log(1/ε))$. Notably, our results extend beyond the PSD setting to general low-rank matrix recovery problem. Numerical experiments further validate that ScaledGD accelerates convergence for ill-conditioned matrices with the optimal sampling complexity.

</details>


### [119] [Breaking Data Symmetry is Needed For Generalization in Feature Learning Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00316)
*Marcel Tomàs Bernal,Neil Rohit Mallinar,Mikhail Belkin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究通过递归特征机算法在代数任务上探索grokking现象，发现泛化仅发生在训练集对称性被打破时，且算法通过恢复数据中的不变性群作用实现泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究grokking现象（模型先达到高训练精度，很久后才泛化到未见测试点）在代数任务上的机制，特别是通过特征学习核方法理解泛化发生的条件。

Method: 使用递归特征机算法，通过平均梯度外积迭代更新特征矩阵来学习任务相关特征，在代数任务上研究grokking现象。

Result: 发现泛化仅发生在训练集对称性被打破时；RFM通过恢复数据中固有的不变性群作用实现泛化；学习的特征矩阵编码了不变性群的特定元素。

Conclusion: grokking现象与训练数据对称性密切相关，泛化需要打破对称性，而RFM通过恢复不变性群结构实现这一过程，解释了泛化对对称性的依赖关系。

Abstract: Grokking occurs when a model achieves high training accuracy but generalization to unseen test points happens long after that. This phenomenon was initially observed on a class of algebraic problems, such as learning modular arithmetic (Power et al., 2022). We study grokking on algebraic tasks in a class of feature learning kernels via the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm (Radhakrishnan et al., 2024), which iteratively updates feature matrices through the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) of an estimator in order to learn task-relevant features. Our main experimental finding is that generalization occurs only when a certain symmetry in the training set is broken. Furthermore, we empirically show that RFM generalizes by recovering the underlying invariance group action inherent in the data. We find that the learned feature matrices encode specific elements of the invariance group, explaining the dependence of generalization on symmetry.

</details>


### [120] [Denoising distances beyond the volumetric barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00432)
*Han Huang,Pakawut Jiradilok,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ORDER方法，在随机几何图中实现点对距离估计精度达到n^{-2/(d+5)}，突破维度d>5时的体积障碍限制n^{-1/d}，重构的度量空间与真实流形的Gromov-Wasserstein距离达到n^{-1/d}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机几何图流形重构方法受限于体积障碍（volumetric barrier），即点对距离估计精度受限于样本间距尺度n^{-1/d}。需要突破这一根本限制，实现更精确的距离估计。

Method: 提出正交环距离估计方法（ORDER），通过创新算法在多项式时间内实现点对距离估计精度达到n^{-2/(d+5)}（忽略对数因子），严格超越体积障碍限制。

Result: 1. 点对距离估计精度达到n^{-2/(d+5)}，当d>5时严格优于体积障碍n^{-1/d}；2. 重构度量空间与真实流形的Gromov-Wasserstein距离达到n^{-1/d}，与经验测度的Wasserstein收敛率匹配；3. 方法适用于一般噪声距离模型、稀疏随机几何图和未知连接概率函数。

Conclusion: ORDER方法突破了随机几何图流形重构中的体积障碍限制，实现了超越样本间距尺度的距离估计精度，重构的度量空间质量与拥有完整距离矩阵相当，为高维流形学习提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of reconstructing the latent geometry of a $d$-dimensional Riemannian manifold from a random geometric graph. While recent works have made significant progress in manifold recovery from random geometric graphs, and more generally from noisy distances, the precision of pairwise distance estimation has been fundamentally constrained by the volumetric barrier, namely the natural sample-spacing scale $n^{-1/d}$ coming from the fact that a generic point of the manifold typically lies at distance of order $n^{-1/d}$ from the nearest sampled point. In this paper, we introduce a novel approach, Orthogonal Ring Distance Estimation Routine (ORDER), which achieves a pointwise distance estimation precision of order $n^{-2/(d+5)}$ up to polylogarithmic factors in $n$ in polynomial time. This strictly beats the volumetric barrier for dimensions $d > 5$.
  As a consequence of obtaining pointwise precision better than $n^{-1/d}$, we prove that the Gromov--Wasserstein distance between the reconstructed metric measure space and the true latent manifold is of order $n^{-1/d}$. This matches the Wasserstein convergence rate of empirical measures, demonstrating that our reconstructed graph metric is asymptotically as good as having access to the full pairwise distance matrix of the sampled points. Our results are proven in a very general setting which includes general models of noisy pairwise distances, sparse random geometric graphs, and unknown connection probability functions.

</details>


### [121] [Scenario theory for multi-criteria data-driven decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00553)
*Simone Garatti,Lucrezia Manieri,Alessandro Falsone,Algo Carè,Marco C. Campi,Maria Prandini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出多准则场景理论，用于多准则数据驱动决策，通过集体处理各准则违反风险，获得比传统方法更精确的鲁棒性保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景方法主要针对基于单一数据集评估单一准则的鲁棒性，而实际应用（如多智能体决策）需要同时考虑多个准则，并基于多个数据集（每个准则一个）评估鲁棒性

Method: 开发多准则数据驱动决策的通用场景理论，核心创新在于集体处理各准则违反风险，而非单独处理，从而获得更精确的鲁棒性证书

Result: 提出的框架能更准确地量化所有准则同时满足的鲁棒性水平，相比传统方法提供更精确的鲁棒性保证

Conclusion: 该框架为多准则数据驱动决策问题提供了原则性、可扩展且理论可靠的不确定性设计方法，适用于广泛的多准则决策场景

Abstract: The scenario approach provides a powerful data-driven framework for designing solutions under uncertainty with rigorous probabilistic robustness guarantees. Existing theory, however, primarily addresses assessing robustness with respect to a single appropriateness criterion for the solution based on a dataset, whereas many practical applications - including multi-agent decision problems - require the simultaneous consideration of multiple criteria and the assessment of their robustness based on multiple datasets, one per criterion. This paper develops a general scenario theory for multi-criteria data-driven decision making. A central innovation lies in the collective treatment of the risks associated with violations of individual criteria, which yields substantially more accurate robustness certificates than those derived from a naive application of standard results. In turn, this approach enables a sharper quantification of the robustness level with which all criteria are simultaneously satisfied. The proposed framework applies broadly to multi-criteria data-driven decision problems, providing a principled, scalable, and theoretically grounded methodology for design under uncertainty.

</details>


### [122] [Inverse-Free Sparse Variational Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00697)
*Stefano Cortinovis,Laurence Aitchison,Stefanos Eleftheriadis,Mark van der Wilk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于矩阵乘法的无逆稀疏变分高斯过程方法，通过改进条件化的变分界和自然梯度更新，解决了传统方法在低精度并行硬件上的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程在大规模训练时计算成本高，稀疏变分近似虽然能降低成本，但仍依赖核矩阵的Cholesky分解，这不适合低精度、大规模并行硬件。现有的仅依赖矩阵乘法的变分界方法在优化时面临挑战。

Method: 提出改进条件化的变分界，推导出仅需矩阵乘法的自然梯度更新规则来优化辅助参数，并设计了简单启发式方法（如步长调度和停止准则）来提升稳定性和收敛性。

Result: 在回归和分类基准测试中，该方法可作为SVGP模型的即插即用替代方案，恢复与传统方法相似的性能，在良好调优时比基线方法更快。

Conclusion: 提出的无逆方法使稀疏变分高斯过程更适合现代硬件架构，通过矩阵乘法优化实现了稳定高效的训练，为深度高斯过程等模型提供了实用的计算解决方案。

Abstract: Gaussian processes (GPs) offer appealing properties but are costly to train at scale. Sparse variational GP (SVGP) approximations reduce cost yet still rely on Cholesky decompositions of kernel matrices, ill-suited to low-precision, massively parallel hardware. While one can construct valid variational bounds that rely only on matrix multiplications (matmuls) via an auxiliary matrix parameter, optimising them with off-the-shelf first-order methods is challenging. We make the inverse-free approach practical by proposing a better-conditioned bound and deriving a matmul-only natural-gradient update for the auxiliary parameter, markedly improving stability and convergence. We further provide simple heuristics, such as step-size schedules and stopping criteria, that make the overall optimisation routine fit seamlessly into existing workflows. Across regression and classification benchmarks, we demonstrate that our method 1) serves as a drop-in replacement in SVGP-based models (e.g., deep GPs), 2) recovers similar performance to traditional methods, and 3) can be faster than baselines when well tuned.

</details>


### [123] [Deconfounding Scores and Representation Learning for Causal Effect Estimation with Weak Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00811)
*Oscar Clivio,Alexander D'Amour,Alexander Franks,David Bruns-Smith,Chris Holmes,Avi Feller*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种称为"去混淆分数"的特征表示方法，用于改善因果效应估计中的重叠性问题，证明了在广义线性模型下预后分数是重叠最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果效应估计中的重叠性（正性）是关键条件，但许多流行估计器在处理组特征差异大时方差高且脆弱，高维情况下维度灾难使重叠性难以满足。

Method: 提出去混淆分数作为特征表示，保持识别性和估计目标；将寻找更好重叠表示问题形式化为在去混淆分数约束下最小化重叠散度；推导出高斯特征广义线性模型下去混淆分数的闭式解。

Result: 证明在广义线性模型下，预后分数是去混淆分数类中的重叠最优表示；通过大量实验验证了这一理论发现。

Conclusion: 去混淆分数为解决高维因果推断中的重叠性问题提供了理论框架，预后分数作为重叠最优表示具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Overlap, also known as positivity, is a key condition for causal treatment effect estimation. Many popular estimators suffer from high variance and become brittle when features differ strongly across treatment groups. This is especially challenging in high dimensions: the curse of dimensionality can make overlap implausible. To address this, we propose a class of feature representations called deconfounding scores, which preserve both identification and the target of estimation; the classical propensity and prognostic scores are two special cases. We characterize the problem of finding a representation with better overlap as minimizing an overlap divergence under a deconfounding score constraint. We then derive closed-form expressions for a class of deconfounding scores under a broad family of generalized linear models with Gaussian features and show that prognostic scores are overlap-optimal within this class. We conduct extensive experiments to assess this behavior empirically.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [One-step TMLE for weighted average treatment effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00198)
*Yang Liu,Patrick Lopatto,Ivana Malenica*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文分析了针对加权平均处理效应(WATEs)的定向最大似然估计(TMLE)，证明了在特定条件下，沿通用最小有利路径的一步TMLE算法是良定义的，能在有限时间内收敛，并产生渐近有效的估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究加权平均处理效应(WATEs)的估计问题，这类因果估计量使用倾向得分的指定函数对协变量分布进行重加权。该类别包括平均处理效应、对处理组的平均处理效应以及各种基于重叠性的目标参数。需要分析TMLE算法在这类参数估计中的理论性质。

Method: 采用沿通用最小有利路径的一步定向最大似然估计方法。在明确的权重函数和初始化正则条件下，分析目标化过程的性质，证明算法是良定义的，能在有限时间内达到估计方程的解，并产生渐近有效的估计量。

Result: 证明了在特定条件下，目标化过程是良定义的，能在有限时间内收敛到估计方程的解，并且产生的估计量是渐近有效的。目标化动态的收敛性和二阶余项的控制都从这些条件中推导出来，而不是作为算法的单独假设。

Conclusion: 该研究为加权平均处理效应的定向最大似然估计提供了全面的理论分析，建立了算法收敛性和渐近有效性的严格条件，为这类因果推断方法的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider Targeted Maximum Likelihood Estimation (TMLE) of weighted average treatment effects (WATEs), a class of causal estimands that reweight the covariate distribution using a specified function of the propensity score. This class includes the average treatment effect and average treatment effect on the treated, as well as various overlap-based targets. We provide a comprehensive analysis of the one-step TMLE along the universal least favorable path for such parameters. Under explicit regularity conditions on the weight function and initialization, we show that the targeting procedure is well-defined, reaches a solution of the estimating equation in finite time, and yields an asymptotically efficient estimator. In particular, convergence of the targeting dynamics and control of the second-order remainder are derived from these conditions rather than imposed as separate assumptions on the output of the algorithm.

</details>


### [125] [E-Values, Bayes Risk, Dual Role of Markov's Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00337)
*Nicholas G. Polson,Daniel Zantedeschi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了假设检验中e值检验与贝叶斯风险最小化两种方法的对称性，指出似然比并非固有e值，其状态取决于验证期望的实验分布，并揭示了零假设与备择假设在控制两类错误概率时的角色反转对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示假设检验中e值检验与贝叶斯风险最小化方法之间的深层对称关系，阐明似然比作为e值的条件依赖性，以及零假设与备择假设在控制两类错误时的角色反转机制。

Method: 通过马尔可夫不等式分析两类错误概率控制，比较两种方法中验证单位矩条件的分布差异（零假设验证I类错误，备择假设验证II类错误），利用Barron和Clarke（1994）的信息论论证进行渐近锐化，并在Polson等人的类型化证据演算框架中定位这种对偶性。

Result: 明确揭示了假设检验中e值检验与贝叶斯风险最小化之间的角色反转对称性，阐明了似然比作为e值的条件性本质，展示了这种对称性在信息论框架下的渐近锐化，并将其置于类型化证据演算的理论体系中。

Conclusion: 假设检验的两种主要方法本质上是关于零假设与备择假设角色反转的对称对偶，似然比的状态取决于验证其期望的实验分布，这种对称性在信息论框架下得到渐近锐化，并在类型化证据演算中找到了理论定位。

Abstract: Two approaches to hypothesis testing, e-value testing and Bayes risk minimisation, both invoke Markov's inequality to control error probabilities. They differ in which distribution certifies the unit-moment condition: the null for Type I error, the alternative for Type II error. The likelihood ratio is not intrinsically an e-value; it acquires that status only relative to the experiment under which its expectation is certified. This note makes the resulting role-reversal symmetry explicit, traces its asymptotic sharpening through the information-theoretic arguments of Barron and Clarke (1994), and situates the duality within the typed evidence calculus of Polson, Sokolov, and Zantedeschi (2026).

</details>


### [126] [Empirical tail dependence functions in high dimensions: uniform linearizations and inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00585)
*Axel Bücher,Yeonjoon Choi,Katharina Effertz,Stanislav Volgushev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文建立了高维极值推断的理论基础，包括经验尾部依赖系数的均匀线性化、高维中心极限定理和乘数自助法的有效性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维极值分析中，现有方法主要关注极值依赖结构的建模和估计，但缺乏通用的高维极值推断程序。特别是基于秩的经验尾部依赖统计量，其分布近似因秩的随机波动与未知尾部依赖的波动相互干扰而变得复杂。

Method: 建立经验稳定尾部依赖函数的均匀线性化结果，以有限样本概率界形式量化秩线性化误差；在高维渐近框架下，允许维度随有效样本量指数增长；推导高维中心极限定理并证明乘数自助法对经验尾部依赖统计量集合的有效性。

Result: 获得了均匀线性化概率界，允许维度指数增长；建立了高维中心极限定理；证明了乘数自助法的有效性；并将结果应用于尾部依赖参数M估计量的均匀展开和基于尾部依赖函数集合的空间各向同性推断。

Conclusion: 该研究为高维极值推断提供了理论基础，解决了基于秩的经验尾部依赖统计量的分布近似问题，为相关统计推断方法提供了理论保障，并在尾部依赖参数估计和空间各向同性推断中展示了应用价值。

Abstract: The analysis of extremal dependence in high dimensions has recently attracted considerable interest. Existing methodology primarily focuses on modeling and estimation of extremal dependence structures, often supported by concentration bounds for empirical tail quantities. However, comparatively little is known about general inferential procedures in high-dimensional extremes. In this paper, we develop foundational theory enabling inference for methods based on empirical tail dependence coefficients and stable tail dependence functions. These estimators are constructed from ranks, which complicates distributional approximations since the stochastic fluctuations of the ranks interfere with those arising from the unknown tail dependence. We establish uniform linearization results for empirical stable tail dependence functions in the form of finite-sample probability bounds that quantify the error of the rank linearization uniformly over collections of coordinates. Within an asymptotic framework, these bounds allow the dimension to grow exponentially with the effective sample size while preserving the validity of the linear approximation. Moreover, we derive high-dimensional central limit theorems and establish the validity of multiplier bootstrap procedures for collections of empirical tail dependence statistics. We illustrate the usefulness of the results through two applications: uniform expansions for M-estimators of tail dependence parameters and inference for spatial isotropy based on collections of tail dependence functions..

</details>


### [127] [Semiparametric Fisher Information in Models parametrized by a Normed Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00655)
*Telmo Pérez-Izquierdo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在一般赋范空间参数化模型中的半参数Fisher信息，建立了正半参数Fisher信息等价于感兴趣参数的梯度位于伴随得分算子值域中的条件，推广了Van Der Vaart (1991)的关键定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是建立超越希尔伯特空间的统一框架，连接可微性和信息理论，为理解半参数模型中的信息界限和根n估计可能性提供理论基础。

Method: 方法包括：1) 在一般赋范空间中建立半参数Fisher信息的理论框架；2) 证明正半参数Fisher信息与梯度位于伴随得分算子值域的等价性；3) 开发赋范空间均方可微模型；4) 应用于两个典型问题：已知变换的平均值估计和点密度估计。

Result: 主要结果：1) 建立了正半参数Fisher信息与梯度位于伴随得分算子值域的等价性定理；2) 在已知变换平均值估计中，正信息当且仅当变换具有有限方差；3) 在点密度估计中，正信息当且仅当评估点处密度具有正质量；4) 为已知极小极大结果提供了新的信息理论视角。

Conclusion: 结论：该研究推广了Van Der Vaart的关键定理，建立了超越希尔伯特空间的统一框架，阐明了根n估计可能性的条件，为半参数模型的信息理论分析提供了新工具和视角。

Abstract: This paper studies semiparametric Fisher information in models parametrized by general normed spaces. The main contribution is to establish that positive semiparametric Fisher information is equivalent to the gradient of the parameter of interest lying in the range of the adjoint score operator. This result generalizes a key theorem Van Der Vaart (1991) and provides a unified framework linking differentiability and information, beyond Hilbert spaces. The paper develops a normed-space mean-square-differentiable models for two canonical problems: estimation of the average of a known transformation and estimation of a density at a point. In these applications, it shows that positive information holds if and only if the transformation has finite variance and if and only if the density has positive mass at the evaluation point, respectively. These findings offer a novel information-theoretic perspective on known minimax results and clarify the conditions under which root-n estimation is possible.

</details>


### [128] [A General Framework for Computational Lower Bounds in Nontrivial Norm Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00966)
*Runshi Tang,Yuefeng Han,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出一个通过反向检测-估计间隙证明计算下界的通用框架，并将其应用于对称张量谱范数近似，证明存在显著的计算障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术难以近似张量谱范数，但不确定这是技术局限还是真正的计算障碍。需要建立通用框架将检测-估计间隙转化为近似难度的严格下界证明。

Method: 提出通用框架：从检测问题出发，利用估计器误差显著小于计算检测阈值的反向间隙，证明该间隙可转化为底层计算类中任何算法可达到的近似失真下界。将该框架应用于p^d维对称张量的谱范数，结合最近建立的高阶累积张量检测的低度硬度结果和误差低于低度检测阈值的有效可计算估计器。

Result: 证明对于D ≤ c_d(log p)^2的任何低度算法，张量谱范数近似必须产生至少p^{d/4-1/2}/polylog(p)的失真。在低度猜想下，该结论扩展到所有多项式时间算法。该下界在多个重要设置中与已知最佳上界匹配至多对数因子。

Conclusion: 指数d/4-1/2捕捉了真正的计算障碍，张量谱范数近似的困难不是现有技术的伪影，而是反映了更广泛的计算障碍。反向检测-估计间隙框架为证明范数近似的计算硬度提供了通用机制。

Abstract: In this note, we propose a general framework for proving computational lower bounds in norm approximation by leveraging a reverse detection--estimation gap. The starting point is a testing problem together with an estimator whose error is significantly smaller than the corresponding computational detection threshold. We show that such a gap yields a lower bound on the approximation distortion achievable by any algorithm in the underlying computational class. In this way, reverse detection--estimation gaps can be turned into a general mechanism for certifying the hardness of approximating nontrivial norms. We apply this framework to the spectral norm of order-$d$ symmetric tensors in $\mathbb{R}^{p^d}$. Using a recently established low-degree hardness result for detecting nonzero high-order cumulant tensors, together with an efficiently computable estimator whose error is below the low-degree detection threshold, we prove that any degree-$D$ low-degree algorithm with $D \le c_d(\log p)^2$ must incur distortion at least $p^{d/4-1/2}/\operatorname{polylog}(p)$ for the tensor spectral norm. Under the low-degree conjecture, the same conclusion extends to all polynomial-time algorithms. In several important settings, this lower bound matches the best known upper bounds up to polylogarithmic factors, suggesting that the exponent $d/4-1/2$ captures a genuine computational barrier. Our results provide evidence that the difficulty of approximating tensor spectral norm is not merely an artifact of existing techniques, but reflects a broader computational barrier.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Differentially Private Manifold Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00942)
*Jiaqi Wu,Yiqing Sun,Zhigang Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种差分隐私流形去噪框架，利用敏感参考数据集校正噪声查询点，同时保护隐私


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管环境中，需要利用敏感参考数据进行流形分析（如嵌入、聚类、可视化），但必须保护参考数据的隐私。现有流形方法缺乏正式的差分隐私保证。

Method: 迭代框架：(1) 在校准敏感度下使用参考数据私有估计局部均值和切向几何；(2) 沿私有估计子空间将查询点投影向局部均值；(3) 使用(ε,δ)-差分隐私进行严格的隐私核算。框架模块化且可扩展，分离DP保护的局部几何与查询点更新。

Result: 在流形正则性、采样密度和测量噪声的标准假设下，建立了高概率效用保证，显示校正查询以非渐近速率收敛到流形。仿真和案例研究表明，在适度隐私预算下能准确恢复信号，展示了清晰的效用-隐私权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架将差分隐私引入流形方法，为参考数据提供正式DP保证，同时保留足够的几何信号用于下游任务。为受监管环境中的流形工作流程提供了可部署的DP组件，无需重新设计隐私系统。

Abstract: We introduce a differentially private manifold denoising framework that allows users to exploit sensitive reference datasets to correct noisy, non-private query points without compromising privacy. The method follows an iterative procedure that (i) privately estimates local means and tangent geometry using the reference data under calibrated sensitivity, (ii) projects query points along the privately estimated subspace toward the local mean via corrective steps at each iteration, and (iii) performs rigorous privacy accounting across iterations and queries using $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy (DP). Conceptually, this framework brings differential privacy to manifold methods, retaining sufficient geometric signal for downstream tasks such as embedding, clustering, and visualization, while providing formal DP guarantees for the reference data. Practically, the procedure is modular and scalable, separating DP-protected local geometry (means and tangents) from budgeted query-point updates, with a simple scheduler allocating privacy budget across iterations and queries. Under standard assumptions on manifold regularity, sampling density, and measurement noise, we establish high-probability utility guarantees showing that corrected queries converge toward the manifold at a non-asymptotic rate governed by sample size, noise level, bandwidth, and the privacy budget. Simulations and case studies demonstrate accurate signal recovery under moderate privacy budgets, illustrating clear utility-privacy trade-offs and providing a deployable DP component for manifold-based workflows in regulated environments without reengineering privacy systems.

</details>


### [130] [Two-Stage Optimizer-Aware Online Data Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00001)
*Fangxin Wang,Peyman Baghershahi,Langzhou He,Henry Peng Zou,Sourav Medya,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用于大语言模型在线微调的优化器感知梯度数据选择框架，通过两阶段过滤加权算法提升收敛速度和下游性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的数据选择方法主要针对离线场景设计，不适合在线微调中数据顺序到达、样本效用随时间变化、自适应优化器影响更新几何的情况

Method: 提出优化器感知框架，将在线选择视为在优化器状态下塑造目标导向更新；建立与二阶目标效用的联系；开发两阶段Filter-then-Weight算法：先过滤几何有用候选样本，再优化其系数；引入因子化外积梯度表示和优化矩阵计算以支持长上下文数据

Result: 实验表明，在相同数据预算下，该方法相比现有在线数据选择基线能持续改善收敛速度和下游性能

Conclusion: 提出的优化器感知框架为LLM在线微调提供了有效的梯度数据选择和重加权方法，通过考虑优化器状态和样本间相互作用，显著提升了在线学习效率

Abstract: Gradient-based data selection offers a principled framework for estimating sample utility in large language model (LLM) fine-tuning, but existing methods are mostly designed for offline settings. They are therefore less suited to online fine-tuning, where data arrives sequentially, sample utility is step-dependent, and the effective update geometry is shaped by adaptive optimizers. We propose an optimizer-aware framework for gradient-based online data selection and reweighting in LLM fine-tuning. Our key idea is to view online selection not as static sample ranking, but as shaping the next target-oriented update under the optimizer state. We formulate this as an optimizer-aware update-matching problem, establish its connection to second-order target utility, and show why subset-level construction must account for interactions and redundancy among selected samples. Based on this view, we develop a two-stage Filter-then-Weight algorithm that first filters geometrically useful candidates and then optimizes their coefficients. To make the framework practical for LLMs, we introduce a factorized outer-product gradient representation and optimized matrix computations for long-context data. Experiments show that our method consistently improves convergence and downstream performance over existing online data selection baselines under the same data budget.

</details>


### [131] [Task-Centric Personalized Federated Fine-Tuning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00050)
*Gabriel U. Talasso,Meghdad Kurmanji,Allan M. de Souza,Nicholas D. Lane,Leandro A. Villas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedRouter：基于聚类的个性化联邦学习方法，通过为每个任务而非每个客户端构建专用模型，解决任务异构性和任务间干扰问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化联邦学习(pFL)方法虽然能提升本地性能，但在两个方面缺乏鲁棒性：(1) 泛化能力：当客户端需要对未见任务进行预测或面临数据分布变化时；(2) 客户端内任务干扰：当单个客户端数据包含多个可能相互干扰的分布时

Method: FedRouter采用基于聚类的pFL方法，通过适配器(adapters)实现模型个性化。使用两种聚类机制：本地聚类将适配器与任务数据样本关联，全局聚类将不同客户端中相似的适配器关联以构建任务中心的个性化模型。还提出评估路由器机制，基于创建的聚类将测试样本路由到最佳适配器

Result: 在多任务数据集上的实验表明，FedRouter在这些挑战性场景中表现出强大的韧性：在任务干扰情况下相对性能提升高达6.1%，在泛化评估中相对改进高达136%

Conclusion: FedRouter通过为任务而非客户端构建专用模型，有效解决了传统pFL方法的泛化能力和任务干扰问题，在异构任务联邦学习场景中表现出优越性能

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising technique for training language models on distributed and private datasets of diverse tasks. However, aggregating models trained on heterogeneous tasks often degrades the overall performance of individual clients. To address this issue, Personalized FL (pFL) aims to create models tailored for each client's data distribution. Although these approaches improve local performance, they usually lack robustness in two aspects: (i) generalization: when clients must make predictions on unseen tasks, or face changes in their data distributions, and (ii) intra-client tasks interference: when a single client's data contains multiple distributions that may interfere with each other during local training. To tackle these two challenges, we propose FedRouter, a clustering-based pFL that builds specialized models for each task rather than for each client. FedRouter uses adapters to personalize models by employing two clustering mechanisms to associate adapters with specific tasks. A local clustering that associate adapters with task data samples and a global one that associates similar adapters from different clients to construct task-centric personalized models. Additionally, we propose an evaluation router mechanism that routes test samples to the best adapter based on the created clusters. Experiments comparing our method with existing approaches across a multitask dataset, FedRouter demonstrate strong resilience in these challenging scenarios performing up to 6.1% relatively better under tasks interference and up to 136% relative improvement under generalization evaluation.

</details>


### [132] [Evolution Strategies for Deep RL pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00066)
*Adrian Martínez,Ananya Gupta,Hanka Goralija,Mario Rico,Saúl Fenollosa,Tamar Alphaidze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ES与DRL性能对比研究：ES在简单任务中可作为DRL预训练辅助，但在复杂任务中训练效率与DRL相当或更差，且预训练效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽然性能强大但计算成本高且参数调整复杂，进化策略作为无导数方法更简单易部署但性能通常不及DRL。本研究旨在系统比较两者在不同难度任务中的表现，并探索ES作为DRL预训练方法的可行性。

Method: 在Flappy Bird、Breakout和MuJoCo Walker等不同难度环境中对比ES和DRL的训练性能，并测试将ES作为DRL算法预训练步骤的效果，评估不同参数设置下的训练效率和稳定性。

Result: ES并不总是比DRL训练更快；作为预训练步骤仅在简单环境（Flappy Bird）中有效，在复杂任务（Breakout和MuJoCo Walker）中对训练效率或稳定性改善有限甚至无改善。

Conclusion: ES作为DRL的替代方案在复杂任务中性能不足，作为预训练方法仅适用于简单环境，在复杂场景中应用价值有限。

Abstract: Although Deep Reinforcement Learning has proven highly effective for complex decision-making problems, it demands significant computational resources and careful parameter adjustment in order to develop successful strategies. Evolution strategies offer a more straightforward, derivative-free approach that is less computationally costly and simpler to deploy. However, ES generally do not match the performance levels achieved by DRL, which calls into question their suitability for more demanding scenarios. This study examines the performance of ES and DRL across tasks of varying difficulty, including Flappy Bird, Breakout and Mujoco environments, as well as whether ES could be used for initial training to enhance DRL algorithms. The results indicate that ES do not consistently train faster than DRL. When used as a preliminary training step, they only provide benefits in less complex environments (Flappy Bird) and show minimal or no improvement in training efficiency or stability across different parameter settings when applied to more sophisticated tasks (Breakout and MuJoCo Walker).

</details>


### [133] [Empirical Validation of the Classification-Verification Dichotomy for AI Safety Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00072)
*Arsenios Scrivens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分类器安全门无法在AI系统数百次迭代中保持可靠监督，但Lipschitz球验证器可实现零误接受的安全自我改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分类器安全门能否在AI系统长期自我改进过程中保持可靠监督，探索安全自我改进的可行性

Method: 1) 测试18种分类器配置在自改进神经控制器上的性能；2) 评估三种安全RL基线；3) 扩展到MuJoCo基准测试；4) 提出Lipschitz球验证器方法，使用可证明的分析边界；5) 采用球链技术实现无界参数空间遍历；6) 在LLM微调中验证方法有效性

Result: 所有分类器配置和安全RL基线都未能满足安全自我改进的双重条件，即使在控制分布分离度下也失败。Lipschitz球验证器在多个维度上实现零误接受，球链技术使MuJoCo Reacher-v4奖励提升4.31，在Qwen2.5-7B微调中实现234倍单球半径的零安全违规遍历

Conclusion: 分类器安全门无法可靠监督AI系统长期自我改进，但基于可证明边界的Lipschitz球验证器可实现安全自我改进，球链技术能实现大规模参数空间的安全遍历

Abstract: Can classifier-based safety gates maintain reliable oversight as AI systems improve over hundreds of iterations? We provide comprehensive empirical evidence that they cannot. On a self-improving neural controller (d=240), eighteen classifier configurations -- spanning MLPs, SVMs, random forests, k-NN, Bayesian classifiers, and deep networks -- all fail the dual conditions for safe self-improvement. Three safe RL baselines (CPO, Lyapunov, safety shielding) also fail. Results extend to MuJoCo benchmarks (Reacher-v4 d=496, Swimmer-v4 d=1408, HalfCheetah-v4 d=1824). At controlled distribution separations up to delta_s=2.0, all classifiers still fail -- including the NP-optimal test and MLPs with 100% training accuracy -- demonstrating structural impossibility.
  We then show the impossibility is specific to classification, not to safe self-improvement itself. A Lipschitz ball verifier achieves zero false accepts across dimensions d in {84, 240, 768, 2688, 5760, 9984, 17408} using provable analytical bounds (unconditional delta=0). Ball chaining enables unbounded parameter-space traversal: on MuJoCo Reacher-v4, 10 chains yield +4.31 reward improvement with delta=0; on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct during LoRA fine-tuning, 42 chain transitions traverse 234x the single-ball radius with zero safety violations across 200 steps. A 50-prompt oracle confirms oracle-agnosticity. Compositional per-group verification enables radii up to 37x larger than full-network balls. At d<=17408, delta=0 is unconditional; at LLM scale, conditional on estimated Lipschitz constants.

</details>


### [134] [ParetoBandit: Budget-Paced Adaptive Routing for Non-Stationary LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00136)
*Annette Taberner-Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParetoBandit是一个基于成本感知上下文老虎机的开源自适应路由器，能够在运行时动态适应价格和质量变化，同时强制执行美元计价的预算约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产LLM服务通常依赖成本范围约530倍的多模型组合，路由决策需要在质量和成本之间权衡。这种权衡是非平稳的：提供商修订定价、模型质量可能无声退化、新模型需要无停机集成，现有解决方案无法同时满足这些需求。

Method: 采用三种机制：1) 在线原始-对偶预算调节器，在无限请求流上强制执行每个请求的成本上限；2) 充分统计量的几何遗忘，实现快速适应价格和质量变化；3) 热交换注册表，允许运行时添加/移除模型，新模型通过强制探索阶段后由UCB选择发现其质量-成本定位。

Result: 在三个模型组合的1,824个提示路由测试中，七个预算上限下平均每个请求成本从未超过目标0.4%。系统能适应变化：最昂贵模型价格下降一个数量级时质量提升达0.071；无声质量退化能在预算内检测并重新路由；冷启动模型在约142步内达到有意义采用。路由延迟仅9.8ms，路由决策本身仅22.5μs。

Conclusion: ParetoBandit是首个能同时强制执行美元计价预算、在线适应价格/质量变化、并在运行时集成新模型的自适应路由器，通过智能路由而非盲目采用实现了成本控制和质量优化的平衡。

Abstract: Production LLM serving often relies on multi-model portfolios spanning a ~530x cost range, where routing decisions trade off quality against cost. This trade-off is non-stationary: providers revise pricing, model quality can regress silently, and new models must be integrated without downtime. We present ParetoBandit, an open-source adaptive router built on cost-aware contextual bandits that is the first to simultaneously enforce dollar-denominated budgets, adapt online to such shifts, and onboard new models at runtime.
  ParetoBandit closes these gaps through three mechanisms. An online primal-dual budget pacer enforces a per-request cost ceiling over an open-ended stream, replacing offline penalty tuning with closed-loop control. Geometric forgetting on sufficient statistics enables rapid adaptation to price and quality shifts while bootstrapping from offline priors. A hot-swap registry lets operators add or remove models at runtime, with a brief forced-exploration phase for each newcomer, after which UCB selection discovers its quality-cost niche from live traffic alone.
  We evaluate ParetoBandit across four deployment scenarios on 1,824 prompts routed through a three-model portfolio. Across seven budget ceilings, mean per-request cost never exceeds the target by more than 0.4%. When conditions shift, the system adapts: an order-of-magnitude price cut on the costliest model yields up to +0.071 quality lift, and a silent quality regression is detected and rerouted within budget. A cold-started model reaches meaningful adoption within ~142 steps without breaching the cost ceiling. The router discriminates rather than blindly adopting: expensive models are budget-gated and low-quality models rejected after bounded exploration. End-to-end routing latency is 9.8ms on CPU -- less than 0.4% of typical inference time -- with the routing decision itself taking just 22.5us.

</details>


### [135] [Sit-to-Stand Transitions Detection and Duration Measurement Using Smart Lacelock Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00175)
*Md Rafi Islam,Md Rejwanul Haque,Elizabeth Choma,Shannon Hayes,Siobhan McMahon,Xiangrong Shen,Edward Sazonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用鞋载传感器Smart Lacelock检测老年人坐站转换动作并测量其持续时间，通过机器学习分类器实现高精度识别，为跌倒风险评估提供可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坐站转换动作是评估老年人下肢力量、肌肉骨骼健康和跌倒风险的关键指标，但现有评估方法存在局限。本研究旨在开发一种基于可穿戴传感器的自动化检测方法，用于实时监测老年人的功能能力和身体衰退。

Method: 使用Smart Lacelock传感器（集成载荷传感器、加速度计和陀螺仪）采集16名老年人在短身体性能电池测试中的坐站转换数据。从多模态信号中提取特征，采用4折参与者独立交叉验证训练和评估四种机器学习分类器，包括袋装树分类器，用于分类坐站转换并测量其持续时间。

Result: 袋装树分类器在坐站转换分类中达到0.98的准确率和0.8的F1分数。正确分类转换的持续时间测量平均绝对误差为0.047秒，标准差为0.07秒。结果表明传感器在现实世界跌倒风险评估中具有应用潜力。

Conclusion: Smart Lacelock传感器结合机器学习方法能够准确检测和测量老年人的坐站转换动作，为临床和社区环境中的实时跌倒风险评估和移动性监测提供了可行的技术解决方案。

Abstract: Postural stability during movement is fundamental to independent living, fall prevention, and overall health, particularly among older adults who experience age-related declines in balance, muscle strength, and mobility. Among daily functional activities, the Sit-to-Stand (SiSt) transition is a critical indicator of lower-limb strength, musculoskeletal health, and fall risk, making it an essential parameter for assessing functional capacity and monitoring physical decline in aging populations. This study presents a methodology SiSt transition detection and duration measurement using the Smart Lacelock sensor, a lightweight, shoe-mounted device that integrates a load cell, accelerometer, and gyroscope for motion analysis. The methodology was evaluated in 16 older adults (age: mean: 76.84, SD: 3.45 years) performing SiSt tasks within the Short Physical Performance Battery (SPPB) protocol. Features extracted from multimodal signals were used to train and evaluate four machine learning classifiers using a 4-fold participant-independent cross-validation to classify SiSt transitions and measure their duration. The bagged tree classifier achieved an accuracy of 0.98 and an F1 score of 0.8 in classifying SiSt transition. The mean absolute error in duration measurement of the correctly classified transitions was 0.047, and the SD was 0.07 seconds. These findings highlight the potential of the Smart Lacelock sensor for real-world fall-risk assessment and mobility monitoring in older adults.

</details>


### [136] [Lévy-Flow Models: Heavy-Tail-Aware Normalizing Flows for Financial Risk Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00195)
*Rachid Drissi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lévy-Flows是一种新型归一化流模型，用基于Lévy过程的分布（方差伽马和正态逆高斯）替代标准高斯基分布，能有效建模重尾数据并保持精确似然评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于高斯分布的归一化流在建模金融数据等重尾分布时存在局限，需要能自然捕捉重尾行为同时保持计算效率的替代方案。

Method: 提出Lévy-Flows模型，用Lévy过程分布（方差伽马VG和正态逆高斯NIG）作为基分布替代高斯分布，建立理论保证尾行为特性，并设计identity-tail神经样条流架构。

Result: 在S&P 500日收益率等金融数据上，VG-based流相比高斯流将测试负对数似然降低69%，实现精确的95% VaR校准；NIG-based流提供最准确的期望损失估计。

Conclusion: 将Lévy过程结构融入归一化流能显著提升重尾数据建模能力，在金融风险管理等应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: We introduce Lévy-Flows, a class of normalizing flow models that replace the standard Gaussian base distribution with Lévy process-based distributions, specifically Variance Gamma (VG) and Normal-Inverse Gaussian (NIG). These distributions naturally capture heavy-tailed behavior while preserving exact likelihood evaluation and efficient reparameterized sampling. We establish theoretical guarantees on tail behavior, showing that for regularly varying bases the tail index is preserved under asymptotically linear flow transformations, and that identity-tail Neural Spline Flow architectures preserve the base distribution's tail shape exactly outside the transformation region. Empirically, we evaluate on S&P 500 daily returns and additional assets, demonstrating substantial improvements in density estimation and risk calibration. VG-based flows reduce test negative log-likelihood by 69% relative to Gaussian flows and achieve exact 95% VaR calibration, while NIG-based flows provide the most accurate Expected Shortfall estimates. These results show that incorporating Lévy process structure into normalizing flows yields significant gains in modeling heavy-tailed data, with applications to financial risk management.

</details>


### [137] [QUEST: A robust attention formulation using query-modulated spherical attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00199)
*Hariprasath Govindarajan,Per Sidén,Jacob Roll,Fredrik Lindsten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QUEST注意力机制，通过约束键向量到超球面空间解决Transformer训练不稳定问题，提升模型性能和鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制中查询和键向量的范数可能任意增大，导致训练不稳定，特别是在数据中存在易学习的虚假模式时

Method: 提出QUEST（QUEry-modulated Spherical aTtention）注意力机制，将键向量约束到超球面潜在空间，同时允许单个token灵活控制注意力分布的锐度，可作为标准注意力的即插即用替代

Result: QUEST能够稳定训练，提升模型性能，增强对数据损坏和对抗攻击的鲁棒性，在视觉应用和其他领域都表现出通用性

Conclusion: QUEST通过约束键向量范数解决了注意力机制的训练不稳定问题，同时保持了灵活性，是一种有效的注意力机制改进方案

Abstract: The Transformer model architecture has become one of the most widely used in deep learning and the attention mechanism is at its core. The standard attention formulation uses a softmax operation applied to a scaled dot product between query and key vectors. We explore the role played by norms of the queries and keys, which can cause training instabilities when they arbitrarily increase. We demonstrate how this can happen even in simple Transformer models, in the presence of easy-to-learn spurious patterns in the data. We propose a new attention formulation, QUEry-modulated Spherical aTtention (QUEST), that constrains the keys to a hyperspherical latent space, while still allowing individual tokens to flexibly control the sharpness of the attention distribution. QUEST can be easily used as a drop-in replacement for standard attention. We focus on vision applications while also exploring other domains to highlight the method's generality. We show that (1) QUEST trains without instabilities and (2) produces models with improved performance (3) that are robust to data corruptions and adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [138] [Offline Constrained RLHF with Multiple Preference Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00200)
*Brenden Latham,Mehrdad Moharrami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出离线约束强化学习框架，通过多偏好预言机从人类反馈中学习，在满足受保护群体福利约束下最大化目标群体效用，提供有限样本性能保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对需要在性能与安全性/公平性之间权衡的应用场景，研究如何从人类偏好反馈中学习约束策略，确保受保护群体的最低福利水平

Method: 从参考策略收集的成对比较中通过最大似然估计特定预言机的奖励函数，将约束目标转化为KL正则化的拉格朗日函数，其原始优化器为吉布斯策略，将学习简化为凸对偶问题，提出仅需对偶变量的算法

Result: 提出确保高概率约束满足的对偶算法，为离线约束偏好学习提供首个有限样本性能保证，理论分析可扩展到多约束和一般f-散度正则化

Conclusion: 建立了从人类偏好反馈进行离线约束强化学习的理论框架，提供了统计不确定性传播分析和有限样本保证，为公平性和安全性约束下的策略学习提供了理论基础

Abstract: We study offline constrained reinforcement learning from human feedback with multiple preference oracles. Motivated by applications that trade off performance with safety or fairness, we aim to maximize target population utility subject to a minimum protected group welfare constraint. From pairwise comparisons collected under a reference policy, we estimate oracle-specific rewards via maximum likelihood and analyze how statistical uncertainty propagates through the dual program. We cast the constrained objective as a KL-regularized Lagrangian whose primal optimizer is a Gibbs policy, reducing learning to a convex dual problem. We propose a dual-only algorithm that ensures high-probability constraint satisfaction and provide the first finite-sample performance guarantees for offline constrained preference learning. Finally, we extend our theoretical analysis to accommodate multiple constraints and general f-divergence regularization.

</details>


### [139] [SYNTHONY: A Stress-Aware, Intent-Conditioned Agent for Deep Tabular Generative Models Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00293)
*Hochan Son,Xiaofeng Lin,Jason Ni,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SYNTHONY框架，通过压力特征分析和能力注册表匹配，解决表格数据生成模型选择问题，实现基于用户意图的合成器自动选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据生成模型（GANs、扩散模型、LLM生成器）在不同数据集上表现不稳定，最佳模型选择高度依赖于数据分布特征（如长尾分布、高基数分类、Zipfian不平衡、小样本等）。这种脆弱性使得实际部署困难，用户需要在保真度、隐私性和实用性之间权衡。

Method: 提出压力特征分析（stress profiling）方法，量化数据集在四个可解释压力维度上的难度；开发SYNTHONY选择框架，将压力特征与校准后的合成器家族能力注册表进行匹配；使用kNN选择器基于压力特征预测合成器性能。

Result: 在7个数据集、10个合成器、3种意图的基准测试中，基于压力特征的元特征能高度预测合成器性能：kNN选择器实现了强大的Top-1选择准确率，显著优于零样本LLM选择器和随机基线。分析发现手工制作的能力注册表是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 压力特征分析是有效的合成器选择方法，SYNTHONY框架能根据用户意图选择最佳表格数据生成模型。未来工作需要学习能力表示而非手工制作注册表，以进一步提高选择性能。

Abstract: Deep generative models for tabular data (GANs, diffusion models, and LLM-based generators) exhibit highly non-uniform behavior across datasets; the best-performing synthesizer family depends strongly on distributional stressors such as long-tailed marginals, high-cardinality categorical, Zipfian imbalance, and small-sample regimes. This brittleness makes practical deployment challenging, especially when users must balance competing objectives of fidelity, privacy, and utility. We study {intent-conditioned tabular synthesis selection}: given a dataset and a user intent expressed as a preference over evaluation metrics, the goal is to select a synthesizer that minimizes regret relative to an intent-specific oracle. We propose {stress profiling}, a synthesis-specific meta-feature representation that quantifies dataset difficulty along four interpretable stress dimensions, and integrate it into {SYNTHONY}, a selection framework that matches stress profiles against a calibrated capability registry of synthesizer families. Across a benchmark of 7 datasets, 10 synthesizers, and 3 intents, we demonstrate that stress-based meta-features are highly predictive of synthesizer performance: a $k$NN selector using these features achieves strong Top-1 selection accuracy, substantially outperforming zero-shot LLM selectors and random baselines. We analyze the gap between meta-feature-based and capability-based selection, identifying the hand-crafted capability registry as the primary bottleneck and motivating learned capability representations as a direction for future work.

</details>


### [140] [Lead Zirconate Titanate Reservoir Computing for Classification of Written and Spoken Digits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00207)
*Thomas Buckley,Leslie Schumm,Manor Askenazi,Edward Rietman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究使用未极化PZT陶瓷作为物理储层计算基底处理手写和语音数字分类任务，在MNIST数据集上表现优于线性基线，但在AudioMNIST上与基线相当，表明储层计算在中等难度任务中优势最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索物理储层计算在分类任务中的应用潜力，特别是使用PZT（锆钛酸铅）这种半导体兼容材料作为低功耗计算基底，研究其在处理不同类型数据（图像vs音频）时的表现差异。

Method: 使用未极化的PZT立方体作为物理储层计算基底，处理MNIST手写数字和AudioMNIST语音数字数据集。将预处理后的数据输入PZT储层，利用其非线性动态特性进行计算，并与逻辑回归等线性基线方法进行对比。

Result: 在MNIST手写数字分类中，PZT储层达到89.0%准确率，比相同预处理数据的逻辑回归基线提高2.4个百分点。在AudioMNIST语音数字分类中，储层系统（88.2%准确率）与基线方法（88.1%准确率）表现相当。

Conclusion: 物理储层计算在任务难度超过简单线性分类器能力但仍处于储层动态计算能力范围内时表现最佳。PZT作为半导体兼容材料，为低功耗计算提供了有前景的物理基底，特别适用于中等难度分类任务。

Abstract: In this paper we extend our earlier work of (Rietman et al. 2022) presenting an application of physical Reservoir Computing (RC) to the classification of handwritten and spoken digits. We utilize an unpoled cube of Lead Zirconate Titanate (PZT) as a computational substrate to process these datasets. Our results demonstrate that the PZT reservoir achieves 89.0% accuracy on MNIST handwritten digits, representing a 2.4 percentage point improvement over logistic regression baselines applied to the same preprocessed data. However, for the AudioMNIST spoken digits dataset, the reservoir system (88.2% accuracy) performs equivalently to baseline methods (88.1% accuracy), suggesting that reservoir computing provides the greatest benefits for classification tasks of intermediate difficulty where linear methods underperform but the problem remains learnable. PZT is a well-known material already used in semiconductor applications, presenting a low-power computational substrate that can be integrated with digital algorithms. Our findings indicate that physical reservoirs excel when the task difficulty exceeds the capability of simple linear classifiers but remains within the computational capacity of the reservoir dynamics.

</details>


### [141] [Orthogonal Learner for Estimating Heterogeneous Long-Term Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00915)
*Haorui Ma,Dennis Frauen,Valentyn Melnychuk,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LT-O-learners用于估计异质性长期处理效应，通过自定义重叠权重解决低重叠问题，具有正交性和模型无关性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质性长期处理效应估计在个性化决策中很重要，但面临低重叠问题导致估计不稳定和方差大

Method: LT-O-learners：引入自定义重叠权重重新定位学习目标，降低低重叠样本权重，满足Neyman正交性，模型无关

Result: 理论证明重新定位损失等价于加权oracle损失，满足正交性，提供一般误差界，实验验证在低重叠设置下的鲁棒性

Conclusion: LT-O-learners是首个针对HLTE估计的正交学习器，能有效处理长期结果中常见的低重叠问题

Abstract: Estimation of heterogeneous long-term treatment effects (HLTEs) is widely used for personalized decision-making in marketing, economics, and medicine, where short-term randomized experiments are often combined with long-term observational data. However, HLTE estimation is challenging due to limited overlap in treatment or in observing long-term outcomes for certain subpopulations, which can lead to unstable HLTE estimates with large finite-sample variance. To address this challenge, we introduce the LT-O-learners (Long-Term Orthogonal Learners), a set of novel orthogonal learners for HLTE estimation. The learners are designed for the canonical HLTE setting that combines a short-term randomized dataset $\mathcal{D}_1$ with a long-term historical dataset $\mathcal{D}_2$. The key idea of our LT-O-Learners is to retarget the learning objective by introducing custom overlap weights that downweight samples with low overlap in treatment or in long-term observation. We show that the retargeted loss is equivalent to the weighted oracle loss and satisfies Neyman-orthogonality, which means our learners are robust to errors in the nuisance estimation. We further provide a general error bound for the LT-O-Learners and give the conditions under which quasi-oracle rate can be achieved. Finally, our LT-O-learners are model-agnostic and can thus be instantiated with arbitrary machine learning models. We conduct empirical evaluations on synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks to confirm the theoretical properties of our LT-O-Learners, especially the robustness in low-overlap settings. To the best of our knowledge, ours are the first orthogonal learners for HLTE estimation that are robust to low overlap that is common in long-term outcomes.

</details>


### [142] [Diversity-Aware Reverse Kullback-Leibler Divergence for Large Language Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00223)
*Hoang-Chau Luong,Dat Ba Tran,Lingwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DRKL（多样性感知的反向KL散度）来解决RKL在LLM蒸馏中导致学生模型过度自信、降低输出多样性的问题，在保持RKL优化优势的同时改善多样性和尾部对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然反向KL散度（RKL）在大语言模型蒸馏中表现出色，特别是在词汇量大和师生能力不匹配的情况下，但RKL存在结构性限制：它会导致学生模型产生过度自信的预测，降低输出多样性，并且对非目标类别的监督较弱，导致尾部对齐不佳。

Method: 首先分析RKL的梯度，将其分解为目标和非目标分量，发现非目标梯度即使在学生模型已匹配教师时仍会推动目标logit上升。为此提出DRKL（多样性感知的反向KL散度），消除这种梯度效应并加强非目标监督，同时保留RKL的优化优势。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型家族上的广泛实验表明，DRKL持续优于前向KL（FKL）、RKL和其他最先进的蒸馏目标，实现了更好的性能和更优的保真度-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: DRKL成功解决了RKL在LLM蒸馏中的局限性，在保持RKL优化优势的同时显著改善了输出多样性和尾部对齐，为语言模型蒸馏提供了一个更有效的目标函数。

Abstract: Reverse Kullback-Leibler (RKL) divergence has recently emerged as the preferred objective for large language model (LLM) distillation, consistently outperforming forward KL (FKL), particularly in regimes with large vocabularies and significant teacher-student capacity mismatch, where RKL focuses learning on dominant modes rather than enforcing dense alignment. However, RKL introduces a structural limitation that drives the student toward overconfident predictions. We first provide an analysis of RKL by decomposing its gradients into target and non-target components, and show that non-target gradients consistently push the target logit upward even when the student already matches the teacher, thereby reducing output diversity. In addition, RKL provides weak supervision over non-target classes, leading to poor tail alignment. To address these issues, we propose Diversity-aware RKL (DRKL), which removes this gradient effect and strengthens non-target supervision while preserving the optimization benefits of RKL. Extensive experiments across datasets and model families demonstrate that DRKL consistently outperforms FKL, RKL, and other state-of-the-art distillation objectives, achieving better performance and a superior fidelity-diversity trade-off.

</details>


### [143] [Online Reasoning Calibration: Test-Time Training Enables Generalizable Conformal LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01170)
*Cai Zhou,Zekai Wang,Menghua Wu,Qianyu Julie Zhu,Flora C. Shi,Chenyu Wang,Ashia Wilson,Tommi Jaakkola,Stephen Bates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA是一个在线推理校准框架，通过元学习更新校准模块，为语言模型提供有效的置信度估计，显著减少推理计算成本同时保持低错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在测试时扩展虽然能解决高难度任务，但需要极高的计算成本。这种低效性源于后训练语言模型的校准不当以及流行采样技术缺乏校准。需要一种能在分布偏移下提供有效置信度估计的方法。

Method: ORCA结合了符合性预测和测试时训练，引入元学习过程为每个输入更新校准模块。该方法能够处理推理过程中不同阶段思维模式的分布偏移，以及模型开发与部署间的提示分布变化。

Result: 在风险水平δ=0.1时，ORCA在分布内任务上为Qwen2.5-32B节省了47.5%（监督标签）和40.7%（自洽性标签）的计算成本。在零样本分布外设置下，将MATH-500的节省率从静态校准基线的24.8%提升到67.0%，同时保持低经验错误率。该趋势在不同模型系列和下游基准测试中保持一致。

Conclusion: ORCA不仅提供了符合性风险的理论保证，还在不同推理任务上展示了更高的效率和泛化能力。该框架通过在线校准显著减少了大型语言模型的推理计算成本，同时保持性能。

Abstract: While test-time scaling has enabled large language models to solve highly difficult tasks, state-of-the-art results come at exorbitant compute costs. These inefficiencies can be attributed to the miscalibration of post-trained language models, and the lack of calibration in popular sampling techniques. Here, we present Online Reasoning Calibration (ORCA), a framework for calibrating the sampling process that draws upon conformal prediction and test-time training. Specifically, we introduce a meta-learning procedure that updates the calibration module for each input. This allows us to provide valid confidence estimates under distributional shift, e.g. in thought patterns that occur across different stages of reasoning, or in prompt distributions between model development and deployment. ORCA not only provides theoretical guarantees on conformal risks, but also empirically shows higher efficiency and generalization across different reasoning tasks. At risk level $δ=0.1$, ORCA improves Qwen2.5-32B efficiency on in-distribution tasks with savings up to 47.5% with supervised labels and 40.7% with self-consistency labels. Under zero-shot out-of-domain settings, it improves MATH-500 savings from 24.8% of the static calibration baseline to 67.0% while maintaining a low empirical error rate, and the same trend holds across model families and downstream benchmarks. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzekai99/ORCA.

</details>


### [144] [MAC-Attention: a Match-Amend-Complete Scheme for Fast and Accurate Attention Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00235)
*Jinghan Yao,Sam Adé Jacobs,Walid Krichene,Masahiro Tanaka,Dhabaleswar K Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAC-Attention是一种通过重用相似查询的注意力计算来加速长上下文解码的方法，在保持全注意力质量的同时显著减少KV访问和延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码在LLMs中是IO受限的：每个token都需要重新读取不断增长的KV缓存。现有的加速方法要么通过压缩降低保真度，要么通过选择/淘汰限制可访问内容，这两种方法都会影响延迟回忆和长文本生成质量。

Method: MAC-Attention包含三个阶段：匹配阶段在短局部窗口内进行pre-RoPE L2匹配；修正阶段通过重新计算匹配边界附近的小区域来修正重用的注意力；完成阶段通过数值稳定的合并将修正结果与KV尾部的新鲜注意力计算融合。匹配命中时，计算和带宽复杂度与上下文长度无关。

Result: 在LongBench v2（120K）、RULER（120K）和LongGenBench（16K连续生成）上，相比最新的FlashInfer库，MAC-Attention将KV访问减少高达99%，在128K上下文下将token生成延迟降低超过60%，注意力阶段加速超过14.3倍，端到端加速最高2.6倍，同时保持全注意力质量。

Conclusion: 通过重用计算，MAC-Attention实现了既快速又忠实的长上下文推理，该方法与模型无关，可与IO感知内核、分页KV管理器以及MQA/GQA组合使用。

Abstract: Long-context decoding in LLMs is IO-bound: each token re-reads an ever-growing KV cache. Prior accelerations cut bytes via compression, which lowers fidelity, or selection/eviction, which restricts what remains accessible, and both can degrade delayed recall and long-form generation. We introduce MAC-Attention, a fidelity- and access-preserving alternative that accelerates decoding by reusing prior attention computations for semantically similar recent queries. It starts with a match stage that performs pre-RoPE L2 matching over a short local window; an amend stage rectifies the reused attention by recomputing a small band near the match boundary; and a complete stage fuses the rectified results with fresh attention computed on the KV tail through a numerically stable merge. On a match hit, the compute and bandwidth complexity is constant regardless of context length. The method is model-agnostic and composes with IO-aware kernels, paged-KV managers, and MQA/GQA. Across LongBench v2 (120K), RULER (120K), and LongGenBench (16K continuous generation), compared to the latest FlashInfer library, MAC-Attention reduces KV accesses by up to 99%, cuts token generation latency by over 60% at 128K, and achieves over 14.3x attention-phase speedups, up to 2.6x end-to-end, while maintaining full-attention quality. By reusing computation, MAC-Attention delivers long-context inference that is both fast and faithful. Code is available here: https://github.com/YJHMITWEB/MAC-Attention.git

</details>


### [145] [Hierarchical Discrete Flow Matching for Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00236)
*Yoann Boget,Pablo Strasser,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分层生成框架，通过减少需要评估的节点对数量并采用离散流匹配来降低去噪迭代次数，从而解决图生成中计算成本高和生成需要大量函数评估的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于去噪的图生成模型（如扩散和流匹配）存在两个基本限制：计算成本随节点数呈二次方增长，以及生成过程中需要大量函数评估。这些限制阻碍了模型的实际应用。

Method: 引入分层生成框架，通过减少需要评估的节点对数量来降低计算复杂度，并采用离散流匹配技术来显著减少去噪迭代次数。

Result: 实验证明该方法能更有效地捕捉图分布，同时大幅减少生成时间。

Conclusion: 提出的分层生成框架解决了现有去噪图生成模型的计算效率问题，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升了生成速度。

Abstract: Denoising-based models, including diffusion and flow matching, have led to substantial advances in graph generation. Despite this progress, such models remain constrained by two fundamental limitations: a computational cost that scales quadratically with the number of nodes and a large number of function evaluations required during generation. In this work, we introduce a novel hierarchical generative framework that reduces the number of node pairs that must be evaluated and adopts discrete flow matching to significantly decrease the number of denoising iterations. We empirically demonstrate that our approach more effectively captures graph distributions while substantially reducing generation time.

</details>


### [146] [Softmax gradient policy for variance minimization and risk-averse multi armed bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00241)
*Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于多臂老虎机问题中风险感知设置的新算法，旨在选择方差最小的臂而非期望奖励最高的臂，通过softmax参数化和无偏估计实现收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多臂老虎机算法主要关注识别期望奖励最高的臂，但在风险感知场景中，决策者更倾向于选择方差最小的臂以获得更稳定的回报，需要在平均奖励和风险之间进行权衡

Method: 采用softmax策略参数化，提出新算法通过使用当前臂分布的两个独立抽样构建目标函数的无偏估计，选择最小方差（或最小风险）臂，并在自然条件下证明其收敛性

Result: 算法在理论上证明了收敛性，通过数值实验展示了实际行为表现，并为实现选择提供了指导，能够处理一般风险感知问题中的权衡

Conclusion: 该研究为风险感知的多臂老虎机问题提供了一种有效的算法框架，通过最小化方差而非最大化期望奖励，为需要稳定回报的决策场景提供了新的解决方案

Abstract: Algorithms for the Multi-Armed Bandit (MAB) problem play a central role in sequential decision-making and have been extensively explored both theoretically and numerically. While most classical approaches aim to identify the arm with the highest expected reward, we focus on a risk-aware setting where the goal is to select the arm with the lowest variance, favoring stability over potentially high but uncertain returns. To model the decision process, we consider a softmax parameterization of the policy; we propose a new algorithm to select the minimal variance (or minimal risk) arm and prove its convergence under natural conditions. The algorithm constructs an unbiased estimate of the objective by using two independent draws from the current's arm distribution. We provide numerical experiments that illustrate the practical behavior of these algorithms and offer guidance on implementation choices. The setting also covers general risk-aware problems where there is a trade-off between maximizing the average reward and minimizing its variance.

</details>


### [147] [Informed Machine Learning with Knowledge Landmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00256)
*Chuyi Dai,Witold Pedrycz,Suping Xu,Ding Liu,Xianmin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的知识-数据机器学习（KD-ML）框架，通过将数值数据与以粒度知识地标形式表达的知识片段相结合，构建了包含数据优化和知识约束的增强损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据与知识在本质上是互补的：数据是精确的、局部的，通常局限于输入空间的某些区域；而知识是全局的、在更高抽象层次上表达的。这种互补性为构建更强大的机器学习模型提供了机会。

Method: 提出KD-ML框架，将知识表示为信息颗粒并组织为输入-输出信息颗粒的集合（知识地标）。设计了包含两个组件的增强损失函数：数据优化组件和作为粒度正则化器的知识约束组件，通过超参数平衡数据和知识的贡献。

Result: 在两个物理主导的基准测试中，提出的KD模型始终优于纯数据驱动的ML模型。实验还展示了损失函数中超参数的作用，以及数据质量（噪声水平）和知识地标粒度水平对模型性能的影响趋势。

Conclusion: KD-ML为构建基于知识和数据的统一模型提供了有效的框架，通过利用数据和知识的互补性，能够开发出比纯数据驱动方法更优越的机器学习模型。

Abstract: Informed Machine Learning has emerged as a viable generalization of Machine Learning (ML) by building a unified conceptual and algorithmic setting for constructing models on a unified basis of knowledge and data. Physics-informed ML involving physics equations is one of the developments within Informed Machine Learning. This study proposes a novel direction of Knowledge-Data ML, referred to as KD-ML, where numeric data are integrated with knowledge tidbits expressed in the form of granular knowledge landmarks. We advocate that data and knowledge are complementary in several fundamental ways: data are precise (numeric) and local, usually confined to some region of the input space, while knowledge is global and formulated at a higher level of abstraction. The knowledge can be represented as information granules and organized as a collection of input-output information granules called knowledge landmarks. In virtue of this evident complementarity, we develop a comprehensive design process of the KD-ML model and formulate an original augmented loss function L, which additively embraces the component responsible for optimizing the model based on available numeric data, while the second component, playing the role of a granular regularizer, so that it adheres to the granular constraints (knowledge landmarks). We show the role of the hyperparameter positioned in the loss function, which balances the contribution and guiding role of data and knowledge, and point to some essential tendencies associated with the quality of data (noise level) and the level of granularity of the knowledge landmarks. Experiments on two physics-governed benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed KD model consistently outperforms data-driven ML models.

</details>


### [148] [Hierarchical Apprenticeship Learning from Imperfect Demonstrations with Evolving Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00258)
*Md Mirajul Islam,Rajesh Debnath,Adittya Soukarjya Saha,Min Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HALIDE：一种从非完美学生演示中学习教学策略的分层学徒学习方法，通过排名演示质量并建模奖励函数演化来提升预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中学生互动通常是非完美的、演化的：学生探索、犯错、修正策略，并在理解发展中精炼目标。现有方法依赖最优或接近最优的专家演示和固定奖励，无法有效利用这些非完美演示。

Method: 提出HALIDE（Hierarchical Apprenticeship Learning from Imperfect Demonstrations with Evolving Rewards），通过分层学习框架对非完美学生演示进行排名，在多抽象层次建模学生行为，推断高层意图和策略，同时显式捕捉学生奖励函数的时序演化。

Result: HALIDE比依赖最优轨迹、固定奖励或未排名的非完美演示的方法更准确地预测学生教学决策。

Conclusion: 非完美学生演示不是需要丢弃的噪声，而是具有结构化的信号。通过排名演示质量并建模奖励演化，HALIDE能更好地区分瞬时错误、次优策略和有意义的进展，从而更有效地从真实学生互动中学习教学策略。

Abstract: While apprenticeship learning has shown promise for inducing effective pedagogical policies directly from student interactions in e-learning environments, most existing approaches rely on optimal or near-optimal expert demonstrations under a fixed reward. Real-world student interactions, however, are often inherently imperfect and evolving: students explore, make errors, revise strategies, and refine their goals as understanding develops. In this work, we argue that imperfect student demonstrations are not noise to be discarded, but structured signals-provided their relative quality is ranked. We introduce HALIDE, Hierarchical Apprenticeship Learning from Imperfect Demonstrations with Evolving Rewards, which not only leverages sub-optimal student demonstrations, but ranks them within a hierarchical learning framework. HALIDE models student behavior at multiple levels of abstraction, enabling inference of higher-level intent and strategy from suboptimal actions while explicitly capturing the temporal evolution of student reward functions. By integrating demonstration quality into hierarchical reward inference,HALIDE distinguishes transient errors from suboptimal strategies and meaningful progress toward higher-level learning goals. Our results show that HALIDE more accurately predicts student pedagogical decisions than approaches that rely on optimal trajectories, fixed rewards, or unranked imperfect demonstrations.

</details>


### [149] [Learning to Shuffle: Block Reshuffling and Reversal Schemes for Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00260)
*Lam M. Nguyen,Dzung T. Phan,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用LLM引导的程序进化框架发现新的SGD数据洗牌策略，识别出块重洗和配对反转两个核心组件，理论证明这些组件能降低方差和顺序敏感性，实验验证优于标准洗牌方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机重洗牌策略虽然优于循环和单次洗牌方案，但理论指导有限，缺乏设计更优数据排序方案的方法，需要探索超越随机重洗牌的新策略

Method: 使用大型语言模型引导的程序进化框架发现有效的无放回SGD洗牌规则，抽象出块重洗和配对反转两个结构组件，分别进行理论分析

Result: 块重洗在温和条件下严格降低前缀梯度方差常数，提供可证明的改进；配对反转对称化epoch映射并消除主导的顺序依赖二阶项，将顺序敏感性从二次降至三次；数值实验验证理论并展示一致增益

Conclusion: 通过LLM引导的进化框架成功发现超越随机重洗牌的新洗牌策略，识别出的两个结构组件具有理论保证的优化优势，为设计更优SGD数据排序方案提供了新方向

Abstract: Shuffling strategies for stochastic gradient descent (SGD), including incremental gradient, shuffle-once, and random reshuffling, are supported by rigorous convergence analyses for arbitrary within-epoch permutations. In particular, random reshuffling is known to improve optimization constants relative to cyclic and shuffle-once schemes. However, existing theory offers limited guidance on how to design new data-ordering schemes that further improve optimization constants or stability beyond random reshuffling. In this paper, we design a pipeline using a large language model (LLM)-guided program evolution framework to discover an effective shuffling rule for without-replacement SGD. Abstracting from this instance, we identify two fundamental structural components: block reshuffling and paired reversal. We analyze these components separately and show that block reshuffling strictly reduces prefix-gradient variance constants within the unified shuffling framework, yielding provable improvements over random reshuffling under mild conditions. Separately, we show that paired reversal symmetrizes the epoch map and cancels the leading order-dependent second-order term, reducing order sensitivity from quadratic to cubic in the step size. Numerical experiments with the discovered algorithm validate the theory and demonstrate consistent gains over standard shuffling schemes across convex and nonconvex benchmarks.

</details>


### [150] [Autonomous Adaptive Solver Selection for Chemistry Integration via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00264)
*Eloghosa Ikponmwoba,Opeoluwa Owoyele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于约束强化学习的自适应求解器选择框架，在化学反应动力学计算中自动选择隐式BDF积分器或准稳态求解器，在保证精度的前提下实现3倍平均加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性化学动力学计算是反应流模拟的主要瓶颈，现有混合积分策略通常依赖手动调优的启发式方法或基于瞬时局部状态的监督预测器，缺乏对轨迹影响的考虑

Method: 将求解器选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，采用约束强化学习框架，通过拉格朗日奖励函数在线调整乘子，在用户规定的精度容差下最小化计算成本

Result: 在0D均相反应器中实现平均约3倍加速（范围1.11-10.58倍），保持准确的点火延迟和物种分布；无需重新训练即可迁移到1D逆流扩散火焰，实现约2.2倍加速，仅在12-15%的时空点选择CVODE

Conclusion: 提出的强化学习框架能够学习问题特定的积分策略并尊重精度约束，为具有空间异质刚度的多物理系统开辟了自适应、自优化工作流程的途径

Abstract: The computational cost of stiff chemical kinetics remains a dominant bottleneck in reacting-flow simulation, yet hybrid integration strategies are typically driven by hand-tuned heuristics or supervised predictors that make myopic decisions from instantaneous local state. We introduce a constrained reinforcement learning (RL) framework that autonomously selects between an implicit BDF integrator (CVODE) and a quasi-steady-state (QSS) solver during chemistry integration. Solver selection is cast as a Markov decision process. The agent learns trajectory-aware policies that account for how present solver choices influence downstream error accumulation, while minimizing computational cost under a user-prescribed accuracy tolerance enforced through a Lagrangian reward with online multiplier adaptation. Across sampled 0D homogeneous reactor conditions, the RL-adaptive policy achieves a mean speedup of approximately $3\times$, with speedups ranging from $1.11\times$ to $10.58\times$, while maintaining accurate ignition delays and species profiles for a 106-species \textit{n}-dodecane mechanism and adding approximately $1\%$ inference overhead. Without retraining, the 0D-trained policy transfers to 1D counterflow diffusion flames over strain rates $10$--$2000~\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, delivering consistent $\approx 2.2\times$ speedup relative to CVODE while preserving near-reference temperature accuracy and selecting CVODE at only $12$--$15\%$ of space-time points. Overall, the results demonstrate the potential of the proposed reinforcement learning framework to learn problem-specific integration strategies while respecting accuracy constraints, thereby opening a pathway toward adaptive, self-optimizing workflows for multiphysics systems with spatially heterogeneous stiffness.

</details>


### [151] [Robust Multimodal Safety via Conditional Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00310)
*Anurag Kumar,Raghuveer Peri,Jon Burnsky,Alexandru Nelus,Rohit Paturi,Srikanth Vishnubhotla,Yanjun Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CASA是一种简单的条件解码策略，通过利用MLLM内部表示预测二进制安全令牌来增强多模态大语言模型的安全性对齐，无需外部分类器或模态特定微调。
